# Color by Number's pictures, all drawn for McFly. The display builds them in
# (src/games/ColorByNumber.cpp); the iPhone app keeps a copy that its tests
# hold to this file.
#
# A category:  @ id | Title
# A picture:   = id | Title | category
#              then its legend, a symbol and a colour per pair (numbers
#              follow this order), then its rows, "." for an empty cell.
# A blank line ends a picture. The first twelve stay first, in this order.

@ pets | Pets
@ farm | Farm
@ wild | Wild
@ birds | Birds
@ bugs | Bugs
@ dinosaurs | Dinosaurs
@ sea | Sea
@ food | Food
@ nature | Nature
@ science | Science
@ space | Space
@ tech | Technology
@ tools | Tools
@ music | Music
@ sports | Sports
@ games | Games
@ fantasy | Fantasy
@ stage | Stage & Screen
@ things | Things
@ travel | Travel
@ holidays | Holidays
